## assistant

You’re right. **Conditional does not yet match Intro to LR’s structure closely enough.** I built the curriculum, but used a simpler interface.

| Intro to LR | Conditional now |
|---|---|
| Integrated sequence of notes, tools, checkpoints, drills, practice, and capstones | Lesson list with a shared practice screen |
| Learning-path rail visible while working | General section navigation |
| Previous/next actions and lesson contents | Recommended next step, longer lesson pages |
| Shared workspace with reference/tool drawer | Separate reference pages |
| Distinct guided learning experiences | Repeated lesson and question template |

**The assignment lock is shared by both bootcamps.** It is a separate account-access issue.

My recommendation is to preserve the conditional content and rebuild its presentation around Intro’s workspace and integrated action path: **learn → guided activity → skill drill → review → mixed application**, with your three difficulty levels and targeted assignments retained.

I overstated how closely the first release matched Intro. It needs this structural pass.

## My request:
shouldnta havesequence o diffuculty it should allow them tthe stage again the curriculum according to where hte yare atw thehter e hteyaero conditional


## assistant

Yes. **The curriculum should start students where they are, rather than make everyone complete Beginner, then Medium, then Advanced.**

There should be two ways through it:

- **From the beginning:** a guided teaching sequence for someone new to conditionals.
- **From their current stage:** assign the exact skills and difficulty they need, skipping what they already know.

A student could need Beginner Group 3 language, Medium linking, and Advanced contraposition at the same time. Their curriculum should accommodate that uneven skill profile.

Difficulty should be selectable within each skill. The teaching sequence provides direction; it should not force every student through the same difficulty progression.

Understood. **You assign their starting point and activities. Students follow those assignments; they do not select a difficulty level.**

Levels 1–3 describe the demands of individual activities at different points in the curriculum. They are not three complete passes through the course.

You can assign the full curriculum from the beginning, start someone at a later stage, or assign one specific activity to address a gap. The curriculum’s order follows how conditional skills build on one another.

<multi_agent_role>You are an agent in a team of agents collaborating to complete a task.

You can spawn sub-agents to handle subtasks, and those sub-agents can spawn their own sub-agents. All agents in the team, including the agents that you can assign tasks to, are equally intelligent and capable, and have access to the same set of tools.

You can use `spawn_agent` to create a new agent, `followup_task` to give an existing agent a new task and trigger a turn, and `send_message` to pass a message to a running agent.
`send_message` calls may be read by a human, so ensure they are legible. Always put proper spaces between words and/or numbers.
Child agents can also spawn their own sub-agents.

When you provide a response in the final channel, that content is immediately delivered back to your parent agent.
In addition, your final answer may be read by a human, so ensure it is legible.

You will receive messages in the analysis channel in the form:
```
Message Type: NEW_TASK | MESSAGE | FINAL_ANSWER
Task name: <recipient>
Sender: <author>
Payload:
<payload text>
```
You may also see them addressed as to=/root/..., which indicates your identity is /root/...

Note that collaboration tools cannot be called from inside `functions.exec`. Call `spawn_agent`, `send_message`, `followup_task`, `wait_agent`, `interrupt_agent`, and `list_agents` only as direct tool calls using the recipient shown in their tool definitions, such as `to=functions.collaboration.spawn_agent`, since they are intentionally absent from the `functions.exec` `tools.*` namespace. Available tools in `functions.exec` are explicitly described with a `tools` namespace in the developer message.

All agents share the same directory. In detail:
- All agents have access to the same container and filesystem as you.
- All agents use the same current working directory.
- As a result, edits made by one agent are immediately visible to all other agents.

When calling `wait_agent`, prefer longer waits (minutes) to avoid busy polling.

There are 4 available concurrency slots, meaning that up to 4 agents can be active at once, including you.

Full-history forks (`fork_turns` omitted or `"all"`) inherit the parent model and reasoning effort and do not accept overrides. Only set `model` or `reasoning_effort` when explicitly requested by the user, applicable `AGENTS.md` instructions, or skill instructions; when doing so, set `fork_turns` to `"none"` or a positive integer string.</multi_agent_role>

CLAUDE.md\\r\\n\\r\\nThis file provides guidance to Claude Code (claude.ai/code) when working with code in this repository.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Project snapshot\\r\\n\\r\\nLSAT U is a premium ed-tech web app: React 18 + Vite 5 + TypeScript + Tailwind + shadcn/ui, Supabase backend with Edge Functions, hosted on Lovable. The Lovable-connected project is the source of truth; GitHub (`vnjkoxpl-42905/lsatu-684d89db`) is the mirror. Treat the connected project as canonical unless told otherwise.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Second brain (product memory)\\r\\n\\r\\nProduct context, design decisions, pedagogy, and status do NOT live in this repo. They live in the Obsidian vault at `~/ai-os/`. This repo is the code; the vault is the decision layer. Read the vault when starting a session, needing product/design/pedagogy context, or making a decision that touches roadmap or UX. Skip it for trivial code edits.\\r\\n\\r\\nPath sanity check first:\\r\\n\\r\\n- Vault root (the `~/ai-os` directory IS the Obsidian vault): `/Users/joshuaf/ai-os`\\r\\n- Code repo root (this project): `/Users/joshuaf/ai-os/projects/lsatu`\\r\\n\\r\\nIf any older note says `~/Documents/Obsidian Vault/...` or `~/Documents/Claude/02_PROJECTS/LSATU`, treat that as stale — the vault was consolidated into `~/ai-os`. Use the absolute paths above.\\r\\n\\r\\nProtocol is canonical in `~/ai-os/CLAUDE.md` (Session Start / Session End), run via two skills:\\r\\n\\r\\n- **`/pickup`** (start / \\"brief me\\"): reads `wiki/tasks.md` → `wiki/hot.md` → `wiki/dashboard.md` → `wiki/index.md` → latest `wiki/log.md` entry, then gives a 6-8 line briefing (Now / Next / open threads / suggested next) and waits.\\r\\n- **`/wrapup`** (handoff / \\"done for today\\"): proposes which tasks are done (confirm first), strikes them through in `wiki/tasks.md`, refreshes `wiki/hot.md`, appends a dated `wiki/log.md` entry, updates the task anchor, gives a 4-line confirmation.\\r\\n\\r\\n`wiki/tasks.md` is the living ledger of Now / Next / Done; finished work is struck through with a date so a new session never re-runs it.\\r\\n\\r\\nWhere LSATU truth lives in the vault (paths relative to `~/ai-os`):\\r\\n\\r\\nThe vault was rebuilt on 2026-07-16. **Live** pages:\\r\\n\\r\\n- Teaching voice (route ALL student-facing copy through it): `wiki/teaching-voice.md`\\r\\n- The tutoring business (offers, channels, priorities): `wiki/entities/aspiring-attorneys.md`\\r\\n- Hubs: `wiki/tasks.md`, `wiki/hot.md`, `wiki/log.md`, `wiki/dashboard.md`, `wiki/index.md`\\r\\n- A student\'s whole record (transcripts, notes, homework): `students/<name>/` — see `students/README.md` for the primary/mirror rule\\r\\n- LSAT teaching knowledge: `corpus/index.md` (fills by ingest)\\r\\n\\r\\n**Archived** — the pre-rebuild product knowledge is read-only reference at `archive/wiki-pre-rebuild-2026-07-16/domains/lsat-u/`; it is not maintained and may be stale. Re-ingest a page through `raw/` if it becomes load-bearing again rather than editing it in place:\\r\\n\\r\\n- Product context: `…/synthesis/intro-to-lr-product.md` (flagship Main Conclusion bootcamp)\\r\\n- Design system (colors, type, role tokens): `…/synthesis/intro-to-lr-design.md`\\r\\n- Code/source map: `…/synthesis/lsatu-source-map.md` (regenerable from this codebase)\\r\\n- Quality bar all bootcamps finish to: `…/synthesis/bootcamp-quality-bar.md`\\r\\n- Concepts, entities, domain index: `…/concepts/`, `…/entities/`, `…/index.md`\\r\\n\\r\\nRules: `raw/` is immutable input, never edit it. Read knowledge from `wiki/`, not `raw/`. Use the `obsidian` CLI (`search`, `backlinks`, `read`) for graph traversal (needs the Obsidian app open); flat file reads work without it. When saving deliverables to the vault, use `[[wiki-link]]` syntax and `UPPERCASE-HYPHENATED.md` naming.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Commands\\r\\n\\r\\nScripts (from `package.json`):\\r\\n\\r\\n- `npm run dev` — Vite dev server on port 8080\\r\\n- `npm run build` — production build\\r\\n- `npm run build:dev` — build with development mode flags\\r\\n- `npm run preview` — serve the built bundle\\r\\n- `npm run lint` — ESLint flat config (`eslint.config.js`)\\r\\n- `npm run test` — Vitest single run (runner wired, no specs exist yet)\\r\\n- `npm run test:watch` — Vitest watch mode\\r\\n- Single test file: `npx vitest run path/to/file.test.ts`\\r\\n\\r\\nPackage manager: **npm**. `package-lock.json` is canonical. A stray `bun.lock` / `bun.lockb` exists in the repo — ignore it, do not switch tooling.\\r\\n\\r\\n- `npm run typecheck` — `tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.app.json`. Not optional before a push: `vite build` uses esbuild, which strips types without checking them, so a type error ships and auto-deploys. `.claude/safe-push.sh` gates on this.\\r\\n- `npm run test:e2e` / `npm run playwright` — Playwright e2e (`e2e/smoke.spec.ts`).\\r\\n\\r\\nNo Prettier. Formatting is ESLint-only.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Architecture (big picture)\\r\\n\\r\\n- **Routing** — React Router v6. Routes are registered in `src/App.tsx`, pages live in `src/pages/`. `/foyer` is the landing surface. Feature-gated pages are wrapped by `ProtectedRoute`.\\r\\n- **Auth + permissions** — Supabase Auth session plus Lovable Cloud Auth JWT fallback (`@lovable.dev/cloud-auth-js`). Per-feature access flags (`has_practice_access`, `has_drill_access`, `has_chat_access`, `is_admin`, ...) are resolved in `src/hooks/useUserPermissions.ts`. Session death is detected via JWT/401.\\r\\n- **Supabase integration** — client auto-generated at `src/integrations/supabase/client.ts` (Supabase JS v2). DB types are auto-generated — do not hand-edit. Edge Functions (Deno) in `supabase/functions/`: `admin-manage-users`, `analyze-stem-response`, `generate-micro-drill`, `motivation-engine`, `parse-drill-request`, `tutor-chat`, `voice-coach-respond`. SQL migrations in `supabase/migrations/`.\\r\\n- **State** — React Context for cross-cutting concerns (`AuthContext`, `UserSettingsContext`, `QuestionBankContext`, `ThemeContext`, `TimerContext`). TanStack Query v5 for server state.\\r\\n- **Primary navigation — Foyer** — `src/pages/AcademyFoyer.tsx` composes `src/components/foyer/FoyerSidebar.tsx` (state blocks + workspace nav) with `src/components/foyer/FoyerHeroRing.tsx` (3 nodes: Smart Drill, Resume, …230 tokens truncated…mer Motion for animation. Spline for 3D.\\r\\n- **Styling** — Tailwind 3.4 over HSL custom properties in `src/index.css`, surfaced as tokens in `tailwind.config.ts`. `DESIGN.md` is the visual authority; `.impeccable/design.json` is its machine-readable sidecar. Prefer tokens over ad-hoc hex values, and never add a token name whose CSS variable is not defined - three such names (`accent-bronze`, `accent-warm`, `glow-bronze`) shipped broken for five months and silently removed the focus ring from every shared Button.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Conventions\\r\\n\\r\\n- **Git workflow:** Always commit and push directly to `main`. Never create feature branches or pull requests unless explicitly asked. After completing any code change: stage relevant files, commit with a descriptive message, and push to `main`.\\r\\n- **Push via `.claude/safe-push.sh`, not raw `git push`.** Lovable auto-commits to `main` mid-session; a raw push can get rejected or race a concurrent commit. The script fetches, merges origin/main if it diverged (never rebases, never force-pushes), then pushes.\\r\\n- **Never roll `main` back.** Do not use Lovable\'s \\"Revert to commit\\", \\"revert and resend\\", or history restore, and never reset or force-push `main`. On 2026-09-27 an accidental \\"revert and resend\\" (b998596) silently undid three merged PRs across 121 question-bank files, and students saw the wrong questions again. To undo your own work, `git revert <your SHA>` only. If a Lovable dialog offers a revert, dismiss it; if one fires, stop and tell Joshua before repairing anything.\\r\\n- Path alias `@/` → `./src/` (set in `vite.config.ts` and `tsconfig.json`).\\r\\n- TypeScript strict mode is **off** (`strictNullChecks: false`, `noImplicitAny: false`, `noUnusedLocals: false`). Do not flip these without explicit approval — it would cascade into a very large diff.\\r\\n- Lovable-specific plumbing that must not be removed:\\r\\n  - `lovable-tagger` Vite plugin injects `data-lov-id` in dev\\r\\n  - `vite-plugin-pwa` configured with start URL `/foyer`\\r\\n  - Lovable preview Service Worker guard in `src/main.tsx`\\r\\n- Identity rules: email sign-in, profile identity uses **name** (not username), instructor is displayed as **\\"Joshua\\"**, students display their real profile names.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Cross-tool instruction hygiene\\r\\n\\r\\n`CLAUDE.md` is the fuller project source of truth. `AGENTS.md` is the Codex-facing adapter and should stay aligned without becoming a second full copy.\\r\\n\\r\\n- When updating project instructions, update both files as needed: put durable detail here, and keep `AGENTS.md` as a concise adapter.\\r\\n- When creating a Claude skill that Codex should use, also copy it to `.agents/skills/` with its `SKILL.md` and supporting files.\\r\\n- When creating a Claude agent that Codex should spawn, also create a matching `.codex/agents/<name>.toml` and place the converted instructions in `developer_instructions`.\\r\\n- When adding shared knowledge, put it in `docs/`, `references/`, or `templates/` so every tool can read the same source.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Verification (must not skip)\\r\\n\\r\\nAny change to `src/components/` or `src/pages/` is not \\"done\\" until it has been viewed running in the app (dev server + browser tool, e.g. Playwright or chrome devtools MCP), with a screenshot or DOM check as evidence. Do not report a UI change complete on the strength of the diff alone. This has been the single biggest recurring failure across past sessions (repeated \\"it\'s not working\\" / \\"i dont see it\\" corrections) — treat it as a hard gate, not a suggestion.\\r\\n\\r\\n**Responsive fixes carry a wider gate: verify across the full emulate matrix BEFORE pushing, not after Joshua reports it.** That means narrow, mid, and wide viewports plus the sidecar-docked case, every time. A responsive defect that gets pushed as \\"fixed\\" and does not hold costs a second full session — this has already happened (a pushed Home-page fix that did not survive, then 13 screenshots and 8 emulate cycles to actually close it). One matrix pass before the push is cheaper than either.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Product rules (must not regress)\\r\\n\\r\\n- Foyer (`FoyerSidebar` + `FoyerHeroRing`) is the primary navigation. The Foyer additionally hosts a `GooeyDock` quick-launch row well below the orbital ring; this dock is allowed only on `/foyer`. Do not add docks elsewhere.\\r\\n- Inbox is real messaging with back-and-forth threads and PDF attachments, scoped 1:1 per student with admin.\\r\\n- Admin is fully responsive on desktop and mobile.\\r\\n- Do not expose actions users cannot actually perform — no fake analytics, no dead buttons, no misleading placeholders. Prefer truthful fallbacks over fake data.\\r\\n- Onboarding asks for name, not username. Sign-in uses email.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Known gaps (do not invent answers)\\r\\n\\r\\n- Vitest specs exist (80 `src/**/*.test.ts(x)` files as of 2026-07-16); run them with `npm run test`. Playwright is wired with one e2e spec (`e2e/smoke.spec.ts`), run via `npm run test:e2e`.\\r\\n- No Prettier config and no CI workflows. Nothing runs `typecheck`, `lint`, or the tests automatically — `.claude/safe-push.sh` is the only gate, and only for typecheck.\\r\\n- `README.md` is a stub.\\r\\n- `.claude/settings.json` is set up with a permission allowlist for safe dev work (npm scripts, git reads, MCP GitHub reads) and a denylist for destructive ops. No custom `.claude/agents/`. A `SessionStart` auto-pull hook is wired in `.claude/settings.local.json` (runs `.claude/auto-pull-hook.sh`); Codex mirrors it in `.codex/hooks.json`.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Skill / agent / command vault\\r\\n\\r\\nThe full skill/agent/command vault lives in the brain root at `~/ai-os/skills/`, `~/ai-os/agents/`, `~/ai-os/commands/` (split into `claude/` and `codex/` subtrees, indexed by `~/ai-os/skills/INDEX.md`). It is inert storage — nothing here auto-loads. To use a vault skill in this project, symlink it into `.claude/skills/` (Codex: `.agents/skills/`). if (!value) throw new Error(\'Conditional course provider is missing\'); return value; }\\r\\nfunction practiceUrl(unit?: string, difficulty: ConditionalDifficulty = \'beginner\', area?: string) { const params = new URLSearchParams({ level: difficulty }); if (unit) params.set(\'unit\', unit); if (area) params.set(\'area\', area); return `${BASE}/practice?${params}`; }\\r\\n\\r\\ntype NextStep = { mode: \'learn\' | \'practice\'; unitId?: string; label: string; difficulty: ConditionalDifficulty; phase: ConditionalUnit[\'phase\'] };\\r\\ntype PathSelection = { scope: \'course\' | \'area\' | \'skill\' | \'practice\'; area?: ConditionalArea; unitId?: string; start?: string; assignedLevel?: boolean; difficulty: ConditionalDifficulty; phase?: ConditionalUnit[\'phase\'] };\\r\\nfunction difficultyFrom(params: URLSearchParams): ConditionalDifficulty {\\r\\n  const value = params.get(\'pathLevel\') ?? params.get(\'level\');\\r\\n  return DIFFICULTIES.includes(value as ConditionalDifficulty) ? value as ConditionalDifficulty : \'beginner\';\\r\\n}\\r\\nfunction pathSelection(params: URLSearchParams, practiceOnly = false): PathSelection {\\r\\n  const area = params.get(\'area\') as ConditionalArea | null;\\r\\n  const unitId = params.get(\'unit\') ?? undefined;\\r\\n  const phase = params.get(\'phase\') === \'quantifier\' ? \'quantifier\' : \'conditional\';\\r\\n  const explicitPath = params.get(\'path\');\\r\\n  return { scope: practiceOnly && !explicitPath ? \'practice\' : explicitPath === \'course\' ? \'course\' : explicitPath === \'area\' || (!explicitPath && area) ? \'area\' : unitId ? \'skill\' : \'course\',\\r\\n    area: area ?? undefined, unitId, start: params.get(\'start\') ?? undefined, assignedLevel: params.has(\'level\') || params.has(\'pathLevel\'), difficulty: difficultyFrom(params), phase };\\r\\n}\\r\\nfunction nextSelectedStep(progress: ConditionalProgress, units: ConditionalUnit[], selection: PathSelection): NextStep | null {\\r\\n  const startIndex = selection.start ? Math.max(0, units.findIndex(unit => unit.id === selection.start)) : 0;\\r\\n  const selectedUnits = units.slice(startIndex).filter(unit => (!(selection.scope === \'skill\' || selection.scope === \'practice\') || !selection.unitId || unit.id === selection.unitId) && (!selection.area || unit.area === selection.area) &&\\r\\n    (selection.scope !== \'practice\' || selection.unitId || selection.area || unit.phase === selection.phase));\\r\\n  for (const unit of selectedUnits) {\\r\\n    const difficulty = selection.assignedLevel ? selection.difficulty : getCurriculumDifficulty(unit);\\r\\n    if (selection.scope !== \'practice\') {\\r\\n      if (!progress.lessons[unit.id]) return { mode: \'learn\', unitId: unit.id, label: `Learn: ${unit.title}`, difficulty, phase: unit.phase };\\r\\n      if (!progress.attempts.some(attempt => attempt.questionId === unit.guidedExample.id)) return { mode: \'learn\', unitId: unit.id, label: `Guided activity: ${unit.title}`, difficulty, phase: unit.phase };\\r\\n    }\\r\\n    if (unit.drills[difficulty].some(question => !progress.attempts.some(attempt => attempt.questionId === question.id && attempt.difficulty === difficulty))) {\\r\\n      return { mode: \'practice\', unitId: unit.id, label: `${levelLabel(difficulty)} skill drill: ${unit.title}`, difficulty, phase: unit.phase };\\r\\n    }\\r\\n  }\\r\\n  return null;\\r\\n}\\r\\nfunction nextStepUrl(next: NextStep, selection: PathSelection) {\\r\\n  const params = new URLSearchParams({ phase: next.phase });\\r\\n  if (selection.assignedLevel) params.set(\'level\', next.difficulty);\\r\\n  if (selection.start) params.set(\'start\', selection.start);\\r\\n  if (selection.scope === \'course\') params.set(\'path\', \'course\');\\r\\n  else if (selection.scope !== \'practice\') { params.set(\'path\', selection.scope); if (selection.assignedLevel) params.set(\'pathLevel\', selection.difficulty); }\\r\\n  if (selection.area) params.set(\'area\', selection.area);\\r\\n  if (next.unitId) params.set(\'unit\', next.unitId);\\r\\n  const route = next.mode === \'learn\' ? `${BASE}/${next.label.startsWith(\'Guided activity:\') ? \'activities\' : \'lessons\'}/${next.unitId}` : `${BASE}/practice`;\\r\\n  return `${route}?${params}`;\\r\\n}\\r\\nfunction pathUrl(selection: PathSelection) {\\r\\n  const params = new URLSearchParams();\\r\\n  if (selection.start) params.set(\'start\', selection.start);\\r\\n  if (selection.area) params.set(\'area\', selection.area);\\r\\n  if (selection.assignedLevel) params.set(\'level\', selection.difficulty);\\r\\n  return params.size ? `${BASE}?${params}` : BASE;\\r\\n}\\r\\nfunction RecommendedForward({ selection }: { selection?: PathSelection }) {\\r\\n  const { savedProgress, status } = useCourse();\\r\\n  const [params] = useSearchParams();\\r\\n  const location = useLocation();\\r\\n  const target = selection ?? pathSelection(params, /\\\\/(practice|simulator|hard-sentences)$/.test(location.pathname));\\r\\n  const next = savedProgress ? nextSelectedStep(savedProgress, conditionalUnits, target) : null;\\r\\n  return <div className=\\"cb-card\\"><span className=\\"cb-label\\">Recommended next step</span>\\r\\n    <h2>{next ? next.label : \'You have worked through the selected steps.\'}</h2>\\r\\n    {status !== \'saved\' && <p className=\\"cb-muted\\">Your recommendation updates after your latest work is saved.</p>}\\r\\n    {next ? <Link className=\\"cb-button cb-primary\\" to={nextStepUrl(next, target)}>Continue: {next.label} <ArrowRight size={16} /></Link>\\r\\n      : <Link className=\\"cb-button\\" to={pathUrl(target)}>Return to your curriculum</Link>}\\r\\n  </div>;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport function ConditionalBootcampRoutes({ userId }: { userId: string }) {\\r\\n  const course = useConditionalProgress(userId);\\r\\n  return <Context.Provider value={course}><Routes><Route element={<Shell />}>\\r\\n    <Route index element={<LearningPath />} />\\r\\n    <Route path=\\"lessons/:unitId\\" element={<Lesson />} />\\r\\n    <Route path=\\"activities/:unitId\\" element={<GuidedActivity />} />\\r\\n    <Route path=\\"practice\\" element={<Practice />} />\\r\\n    <Route path=\\"simulator\\" element={<Practice simulator />} />\\r\\n    <Route path=\\"hard-sentences\\" element={<Practice hardLanguage />} />\\r\\n    <Route path=\\"reference\\" element={<Reference />} />\\r\\n    <Route path=\\"indicators\\" element={<Reference indicators />} />\\r\\n    <Route path=\\"flashcards\\" element={<Flashcards />} />\\r\\n    <Route path=\\"diagnostics\\" element={<Diagnostics />} />\\r\\n    <Route path=\\"review\\" element={<Diagnostics review />} />\\r\\n    <Route path=\\"journal\\" element={<Journal />} />\\r\\n    <Route path=\\"settings\\" element={<Settings />} />\\r\\n    <Route path=\\"*\\" element={<Navigate to={BASE} replace />} />\\r\\n  </Route></Routes></Context.Provider>;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nfunction unitSelection(params: URLSearchParams, unit: ConditionalUnit): PathSelection {\\r\\n  const selection = pathSelection(params);\\r\\n  return { ...selection, difficulty: selection.assignedLevel ? selection.difficulty : getCurriculumDifficulty(unit) };\\r\\n}\\r\\nfunction actionUrl(unit: ConditionalUnit, action: \'lesson\' | \'guided\' | \'practice\' | \'repair\', selection: PathSelection) {\\r\\n  const params = new URLSearchParams();\\r\\n  if (selection.assignedLevel) params.set(\'level\', selection.difficulty);\\r\\n  if (selection.start) params.set(\'start\', selection.start);\\r\\n  if (selection.area) params.set(\'area\', selection.area);\\r\\n  params.set(\'path\', selection.scope === \'practice\' ? \'skill\' : selection.scope);\\r\\n  if (selection.assignedLevel && selection.scope !== \'course\') params.set(\'pathLevel\', selection.difficulty);\\r\\n  if (action === \'practice\') params.set(\'unit\', unit.id);\\r\\n  const route = action === \'practice\' ? \'practice\' : action === \'guided\' ? `activities/${unit.id}` : `lessons/${unit.id}`;\\r\\n  return `${BASE}/${route}?${params}${action === \'repair\' ? \'#repair\' : \'\'}`;\\r\\n}\\r\\n"}'}]

One reasoning move at a time.</p><nav aria-label=\\"Conditional bootcamp\\" className=\\"cb-nav\\">{navigation.map(([route, label]) => <NavLink key={route} end={route === \'\'} to={route ? `${BASE}${route}` : pathUrl(selection)}>{label}</NavLink>)}</nav><details className=\\"cb-curriculum-rail\\" open><summary>Teaching sequence</summary>{areas.map(area => { const group = units.filter(unit => unit.area === area); return group.length ? <section key={area}><h3>{names[area]}</h3>{group.map(unit => <div key={unit.id}><Link aria-current={currentId === unit.id ? \'step\' : undefined} to={actionUrl(unit, \'lesson\', unitSelection(params, unit))}>{progress?.lessons[unit.id] ? <Check size={12} /> : <span>{conditionalUnits.indexOf(unit) + 1}</span>}{unit.title}</Link>{currentId === unit.id && <UnitActions unit={unit} selection={unitSelection(params, unit)} />}</div>)}</section> : null; })}</details></aside><main className=\\"cb-main\\" id=\\"conditional-main\\"><header className=\\"cb-workspace-bar\\"><span className=\\"cb-label\\">Conditional reasoning workspace</span><button onClick={() => setReferenceOpen(true)}><BookOpen size={16} /> Open reference</button><div className=\\"cb-save\\" role=\\"status\\">{status === \'saved\' ? \'Saved to your account\' : status === \'loading\' ? \'Loading your saved work...\' : status === \'saving\' ? \'Saving your changes...\' : status === \'unsaved\' ? \'Changes waiting to save\' : \'Changes are not saved\'}</div></header>{error && <div className=\\"cb-error\\" role=\\"alert\\"><p>{error}</p>{progress ? - Support navigation should preserve assignment parameters.
- Reference drawer needs Escape handling and focus trapping.

Teacher starting-stage browser verification was confirmed by parent.

Matched against either `metadata_json.bootcamp_id`\\r\\n   * (slug, see `bootcampIdOverride`) or `source_id` on a classroom_items\\r\\n   * row of type bootcamp_module.\\r\\n   */\\r\\n  routePath: string;\\r\\n  /**\\r\\n   * Override the slug extracted from `routePath`. The\\r\\n   * current `routePath` is always matched.\\r\\n   */\\r\\n  legacyRoutePaths?: string[];\\r\\n  /**\\r\\n   * The student-facing display name, used only by the locked state.\\r\\n   */\\r\\n  title?: string;\\r\\n  children: ReactNode;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * Per-student bootcamp gate. Admins always pass. Students need a\\r\\n * matching `classroom_items` row of type=\'bootcamp_module\' assigned to\\r\\n * them. If no row exists, render a calm \\"Not assigned yet\\" surface.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * Direct URL hits go through this gate, so a student typing\\r\\n * /bootcamp/principle-flaw won\'t see anything they weren\'t assigned.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport default function BootcampGate({\\r\\n  routePath,\\r\\n  bootcampIdOverride,\\r\\n  legacyRoutePaths,\\r\\n  title,\\r\\n  children,\\r\\n}: Props) {\\r\\n  const { user, authReady } = useAuth();\\r\\n  const { is_admin, loading: permsLoading } = useUserPermissions();\\r\\n  const navigate = useNavigate();\\r\\n  const bootcampId =\\r\\n    bootcampIdOverride ??\\r\\n    bootcampIdFromRoute(routePath);\\r\\n  const explicitRoutePaths = [routePath, ...(legacyRoutePaths ?? [])];\\r\\n\\r\\n  const { data: assigned, isLoading } = useQuery<boolean>({\\r\\n    queryKey: [\'bootcamp-gate\', user?.id, routePath, bootcampId],\\r\\n    enabled: !!user && !is_admin && !permsLoading,\\r\\n    queryFn: async () => {\\r\\n      const { data, error } = await supabase\\r\\n        .from(\'classroom_items\')\\r\\n        .select(\'id, status, source_id, metadata_json\')\\r\\n        .eq(\'type\', \'bootcamp_module\')\\r\\n        .eq(\'student_id\', user!.id)\\r\\n        .neq(\'status\', \'archived\');\\r\\n      if (error) throw error;\\r\\n      const rows = (data ?? []) as Array<{\\r\\n        source_id: string | null;\\r\\n        metadata_json: Record<string, unknown> | null;\\r\\n      }>;\\r\\n      return rows.some(\\r\\n        (r) =>\\r\\n          classroomItemMatchesBootcamp(r, bootcampId) ||\\r\\n          (r.source_id\\r\\n            ? explicitRoutePaths.some((path) => r.source_id === path || r.source_id?.startsWith(`${path}/`))\\r\\n            : false),\\r\\n      );\\r\\n    },\\r\\n  });\\r\\n\\r\\n  if (!authReady || permsLoading) {\\r\\n    return <div className=\\"min-h-screen bg-zinc-950\\" />;\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  if (is_admin || assigned) {\\r\\n    return <>{children}</>;\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  if (isLoading) {\\r\\n    return <div className=\\"min-h-screen bg-zinc-950\\" />;\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  return (\\r\\n    <div className=\\"min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center px-6 py-12 bg-background\\">\\r\\n      <div className=\\"max-w-md text-center space-y-4\\">\\r\\n        <div className=\\"mx-auto w-12 h-12 rounded-full bg-muted flex items-center justify-center\\">\\r\\n          <Lock className=\\"w-5 h-5 text-muted-foreground\\" strokeWidth={1.75} />\\r\\n        </div>\\r\\n        <div className=\\"space-y-1.5\\">\\r\\n          <h2 className=\\"text-lg font-semibold\\">Not assigned yet</h2>\\r\\n          <p className=\\"text-sm text-muted-foreground leading-relaxed\\">\\r\\n            {title ? `${title} ` : \'This bootcamp \'}\\r\\n            isn\'t part of your current path. Joshua can add it from the Teacher Workspace.\\r\\n          </p>\\r\\n        </div>\\r\\n        <Button variant=\\"outline\\" size=\\"sm\\" onClick={() => navigate(\'/classroom\')}>\\r\\n          Back to Classroom\\r\\n        </Button>\\r\\n      </div>\\r\\n    </div>\\r\\n  );\\r\\n}\\r\\nimport { useEffect, useRef } from \'react\';\\r\\nimport { useQuery } from \'@tanstack/react-query\';\\r\\nimport { supabase } from \'@/integrations/supabase/client\';\\r\\nimport { useAuth } from \'@/contexts/AuthContext\';\\r\\nimport { updateClassroomItem } from \'@/lib/classroomItems\';\\r\\nimport { classroomItemMatchesBootcamp } from \'@/lib/bootcamps\';\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * Autosaves bootcamp progress back to the student\'s classroom_items row\\r\\n * for the matching bootcamp_id. []).find((r) =>\\r\\n        classroomItemMatchesBootcamp(\\r\\n          {\\r\\n            source_id: r.source_id,\\r\\n            metadata_json: r.metadata_json as Record<string, unknown> | null,\\r\\n          },\\r\\n          bootcampId,\\r\\n        ),\\r\\n      );\\r\\n      return match?.id ?? null;\\r\\n    },\\r\\n    staleTime: 60_000,\\r\\n  });\\r\\n\\r\\n  useEffect(() => {\\r\\n    if (!itemId || totalDays <= 0) return;\\r\\n    const sorted = [...new Set(completedDays)].sort((a, b) => a - b);\\r\\n    const percent = Math.round((sorted.length / totalDays) * 100);\\r\\n    const allDone = sorted.length >= totalDays;\\r\\n    const signature = `${sorted.join(\',\')}|${allDone}`;\\r\\n    if (signature === lastWriteRef.current) return;\\r\\n\\r\\n    const t = setTimeout(async () => {\\r\\n      try {\\r\\n        const patch: Parameters<typeof updateClassroomItem>[1] = {\\r\\n          metadata_json: {\\r\\n            bootcamp_id: bootcampId,\\r\\n            progress: {\\r\\n              completedDays: sorted,\\r\\n              totalDays,\\r\\n              percent,\\r\\n              lastDayAt: new Date().toISOString(),\\r\\n            },\\r\\n          },\\r\\n        };\\r\\n        if (allDone) {\\r\\n          patch.status = \'completed\';\\r\\n          patch.completed_at = new Date().toISOString();\\r\\n        } else if (sorted.length > 0) {\\r\\n          patch.status = \'in_progress\';\\r\\n        }\\r\\n        await updateClassroomItem(itemId, patch);\\r\\n        lastWriteRef.current = signature;\\r\\n      } catch (e) {\\r\\n        console.warn(\'[useBootcampProgress] save failed\', e);\\r\\n      }\\r\\n    }, 1000);\\r\\n\\r\\n    return () => clearTimeout(t);\\r\\n  }, [itemId, bootcampId, completedDays, totalDays]);\\r\\n}\\r\\nimport { useQuery } from \'@tanstack/react-query\';\\r\\nimport { supabase } from \'@/integrations/supabase/client\';\\r\\nimport { useAuth } from \'@/contexts/AuthContext\';\\r\\n\\r\\nexport type ClassroomItemType =\\r\\n  | \'drill\'\\r\\n  | \'homework\'\\r\\n  | \'activity\'\\r\\n  | \'bootcamp_module\'\\r\\n  | \'note\'\\r\\n  | \'session_recap\'\\r\\n  | \'announcement\'\\r\\n  | \'upload_summary\'\\r\\n  | \'weekly_plan_task\'\\r\\n  | \'smartboard_recap\'\\r\\n  | \'practice_test\';\\r\\n\\r\\nexport type ClassroomItemStatus =\\r\\n  | \'draft\'\\r\\n  | \'assigned\'\\r\\n  | \'in_progress\'\\r\\n  | \'completed\'\\r\\n  | \'archived\';\\r\\n\\r\\nexport interface ClassroomItem {\\r\\n  id: string;\\r\\n  student_id: string;\\r\\n  class_id: string | null;\\r\\n  created_by: string;\\r\\n  type: ClassroomItemType;\\r\\n  title: string;\\r\\n  description: string | null;\\r\\n  content_html: string | null;\\r\\n  content_markdown: string | null;\\r\\n  source_table: string | null;\\r\\n  source_id: string | null;\\r\\n  status: ClassroomItemStatus;\\r\\n  unlock_at: string | Keep the explicit student_id filter even though RLS also protects\\r\\n * student accounts: admins can read every row, and may still open student-side\\r\\n * routes while testing. Without this scope, an admin sees every student\'s work\\r\\n * combined into one calendar.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport function useClassroomItems() {\\r\\n  const { user } = useAuth();\\r\\n  return useQuery<ClassroomItem[]>({\\r\\n    queryKey: [\'classroom-items\', user?.id],\\r\\n    enabled: !!user,\\r\\n    staleTime: 60_000,\\r\\n    refetchOnWindowFocus: false,\\r\\n    queryFn: async () => {"}'}]

## assistant

Existing access is whole-bootcamp only. It does not enforce assigned stages, skills, modes, or activity levels. Intro uses the same broad gate.

Trusted teacher intent already lives in `classroom_items.metadata_json.conditional_target`; links alone are insufficient.

Recommended implementation:

- New Conditional-only assignment reader scoped to the signed-in student.
- Explicitly exclude hidden, draft, archived, and future-unlocked work.
- Validate stored targets, with compatible parsing for older links.
- Derive available notes, guided activities, and drills from those targets.
- Constrain direct student URLs to assigned work.
- Keep unrestricted curriculum outline and target selection in teacher preview.

Avoid `useBootcampProgress` for this: it selects only the newest bootcamp assignment and replaces metadata, which could discard the specific target. No files changed.

unit : unit.id] ?? \'beginner\';\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/** Starting later changes the assigned path without claiming earlier work was completed. */\\r\\nexport function getCurriculumUnits(units: ConditionalUnit[], startingUnitId?: string): ConditionalUnit[] {\\r\\n  const ordered = [...units.filter(unit => unit.phase === \'conditional\'), ...units.filter(unit => unit.phase === \'quantifier\')];\\r\\n  const startIndex = startingUnitId ? ordered.findIndex(unit => unit.id === startingUnitId) : 0;\\r\\n  return ordered.slice(Math.max(0, startIndex));\\r\\n}\\r\\n"}'}]

text(await tools.apply_patch("*** Begin Patch\n*** Add File: C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os-projects/lsatu-conditional-bootcamp/src/bootcamps/conditionals/assignments.ts\n+import { conditionalUnits, findConditionalUnit } from './content';\n+import { DIFFICULTIES, type ConditionalDifficulty } from './content/types';\n+import { getCurriculumDifficulty, getCurriculumUnits } from './curriculumPath';\n+import { CONDITIONAL_AREAS, DEFAULT_CONDITIONAL_TARGET, resolveConditionalAssignment, type ConditionalAssignmentTarget } from '@/lib/conditionalBootcampAssignment';\n+import { classroomItemMatchesBootcamp } from '@/lib/bootcamps';\n+\n+export interface ConditionalAssignmentRow {\n+  id: string; student_id: string; type: string; title: string;\n+  source_id: string | null; metadata_json: Record<string, unknown> | null;\n+  status: string; visible_to_student: boolean; unlock_at: string | null;\n+}\n+export interface ResolvedConditionalAssignment {\n+  id: string; title: string; route: string; target: ConditionalAssignmentTarget;\n+  unitIds: string[]; mode: 'learn' | 'practice'; difficulty?: ConditionalDifficulty;\n+}\n+function parseTarget(value: unknown): ConditionalAssignmentTarget | null {\n+  if (!value || typeof value !== 'object' || Array.isArray(value)) return null;\n+  const target = value as ConditionalAssignmentTarget;\n+  if (!['course', 'stage', 'area', 'skill', 'general'].includes(target.scope) ||\n+    !['learn', 'practice'].includes(target.mode) || !DIFFICULTIES.includes(target.difficulty)) return null;\n+  if ((target.scope === 'skill' || target.scope === 'stage') && !findConditionalUnit(target.unitId)) return null;\n+  if (target.scope === 'area' && !CONDITIONAL_AREAS.some(area => area.id === target.area)) return null;\n+  return target;\n+}\n+function legacyTarget(source: string | null): ConditionalAssignmentTarget | null {\n+  if (!source || !source.startsWith('/bootcamp/conditionals')) return null;\n+  const url = new URL(source, 'https://local.invalid');\n+  const base = '/bootcamp/conditionals';\n+  if (url.pathname !== base && !url.pathname.startsWith(`${base}/`)) return null;\n+  const level = url.searchParams.get('level') ?? 'beginner';\n+  if (!DIFFICULTIES.includes(level as ConditionalDifficulty)) return null;\n+  const difficulty = level as ConditionalDifficulty;\n+  const start = url.searchParams.get('start');\n+  if (start) return parseTarget({ scope: 'stage', mode: 'learn', difficulty, unitId: start });\n+  const pathUnit = url.pathname.match(/^\\/bootcamp\\/conditionals\\/(?:lessons|activities)\\/([^/]+)$/)?.[1];\n+  const unitId = pathUnit ?? url.searchParams.get('unit');\n+  const area = url.searchParams.get('area');\n+  const mode = url.pathname === `${base}/practice` ? 'practice' : 'learn';\n+  if (unitId) return parseTarget({ scope: 'skill', mode, difficulty, unitId });\n+  if (area) return parseTarget({ scope: 'area', mode, difficulty, area });\n+  if (url.pathname === `${base}/practice`) return parseTarget({ scope: 'general', mode: 'practice', difficulty });\n+  return url.pathname === base && !url.search ? { ...DEFAULT_CONDITIONAL_TARGET } : null;\n+}\n+export function conditionalAssignmentUnitIds(target: ConditionalAssignmentTarget): string[] {\n+  if (target.scope === 'course') return getCurriculumUnits(conditionalUnits).map(unit => unit.id);\n+  if (target.scope === 'stage') return getCurriculumUnits(conditionalUnits, target.unitId).map(unit => unit.id);\n+  return conditionalUnits.filter(unit => target.scope === 'skill' ? unit.id === target.unitId\n+    : target.scope === 'area' ? unit.area === target.area : unit.phase === 'conditional').map(unit => unit.id);\n+}\n+export async function resolveConditionalAssignments(rows: ConditionalAssignmentRow[], userId: string, now = Date.now()): Promise<ResolvedConditionalAssignment[]> {\n+  const result: ResolvedConditionalAssignment[] = [];\n+  for (const row of rows) {\n+    if (row.student_id !== userId || row.type !== 'bootcamp_module' || !row.visible_to_student ||\n+      !['assigned', 'in_progress', 'completed'].includes(row.status) || !classroomItemMatchesBootcamp(row, 'conditionals')) continue;\n+    if (row.unlock_at && (!Number.isFinite(Date.parse(row.unlock_at)) || Date.parse(row.unlock_at) > now)) continue;\n+    const metadata = row.metadata_json;\n+    const target = metadata && Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(metadata, 'conditional_target')\n+      ? parseTarget(metadata.conditional_target) : legacyTarget(row.source_id);\n+    if (!target) continue;\n+    const resolved = await resolveConditionalAssignment(target);\n+    result.push({ id: row.id, title: row.title || resolved.title, route: resolved.route, target: resolved.target,\n+      unitIds: conditionalAssignmentUnitIds(resolved.target), mode: resolved.target.mode,\n+      ...(target.scope !== 'course' && target.scope !== 'stage' ? { difficulty: resolved.target.difficulty } : {}) });\n+  }\n+  return result;\n+}\n+/** URL parameters never grant work. Call this against a target read from the student's assignment row. */\n+export function conditionalAssignmentAllowsAction(assignment: ResolvedConditionalAssignment, request: {\n+  unitId: string; action: 'lesson' | 'guided' | 'practice'; difficulty?: ConditionalDifficulty;\n+}): boolean {\n+  if (!assignment.unitIds.includes(request.unitId)) return false;\n+  if (request.action !== 'practice') return assignment.mode === 'learn';\n+  return request.difficulty === (assignment.difficulty ?? getCurriculumDifficulty(request.unitId));\n+}\n+export async function conditionalTeacherPreview(): Promise<ResolvedConditionalAssignment> {\n+  const resolved = await resolveConditionalAssignment();\n+  return { id: 'teacher-preview', title: resolved.title, route: resolved.route, target: resolved.target,\n+    unitIds: conditionalAssignmentUnitIds(resolved.target), mode: 'learn' };\n+}\n*** Add File: C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os-projects/lsatu-conditional-bootcamp/src/bootcamps/conditionals/useConditionalAssignments.ts\n+import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query';\n+import { supabase } from '@/integrations/supabase/client';\n+import { conditionalTeacherPreview, resolveConditionalAssignments, type ConditionalAssignmentRow } from './assignments';\n+\n+export function useConditionalAssignments(userId: string | undefined, isAdmin: boolean) {\n+  return useQuery({\n+    queryKey: ['conditional-assignments', userId, isAdmin],\n+    enabled: !!userId,\n+    staleTime: 0,\n+    refetchOnWindowFocus: true,\n+    queryFn: async () => {\n+      if (isAdmin) return [await conditionalTeacherPreview()];\n+      const { data, error } = await supabase.from('classroom_items')\n+        .select('id,student_id,type,title,source_id,metadata_json,status,visible_to_student,unlock_at')\n+        .eq('student_id', userId!).eq('type', 'bootcamp_module').eq('visible_to_student', true)\n+        .in('status', ['assigned', 'in_progress', 'completed'])\n+        .order('priority', { ascending: false }).order('created_at', { ascending: false });\n+      if (error) throw error;\n+      return resolveConditionalAssignments((data ?? []) as ConditionalAssignmentRow[], userId!);\n+    },\n+  });\n+}\n*** End Patch"));

text(await tools.apply_patch("*** Begin Patch\n*** Add File: C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os-projects/lsatu-conditional-bootcamp/src/bootcamps/conditionals/assignments.test.ts\n+import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';\n+import { conditionalAssignmentAllowsAction, resolveConditionalAssignments, type ConditionalAssignmentRow } from './assignments';\n+import { DEFAULT_CONDITIONAL_TARGET } from '@/lib/conditionalBootcampAssignment';\n+\n+const row = (patch: Partial<ConditionalAssignmentRow> = {}): ConditionalAssignmentRow => ({\n+  id: 'work-1', student_id: 'student', type: 'bootcamp_module', title: 'Assigned work',\n+  source_id: '/bootcamp/conditionals', metadata_json: { bootcamp_id: 'conditionals', conditional_target: DEFAULT_CONDITIONAL_TARGET },\n+  status: 'assigned', visible_to_student: true, unlock_at: null, ...patch,\n+});\n+const targetRow = (target: unknown) => row({ metadata_json: { bootcamp_id: 'conditionals', conditional_target: target } });\n+describe('trusted conditional assignments', () => {\n+  it('resolves course and later starting stage without a course-wide level', async () => {\n+    const [course] = await resolveConditionalAssignments([row()], 'student');\n+    expect(course.unitIds).toHaveLength(30);\n+    expect(course.difficulty).toBeUndefined();\n+    const [stage] = await resolveConditionalAssignments([targetRow({ scope: 'stage', mode: 'learn', difficulty: 'advanced', unitId: 'group-3' })], 'student');\n+    expect(stage.unitIds[0]).toBe('group-3');\n+    expect(stage.unitIds).not.toContain('orientation');\n+    expect(stage.difficulty).toBeUndefined();\n+    expect(conditionalAssignmentAllowsAction(stage, { unitId: 'direct-linking', action: 'practice', difficulty: 'medium' })).toBe(true);\n+    expect(conditionalAssignmentAllowsAction(stage, { unitId: 'direct-linking', action: 'practice', difficulty: 'advanced' })).toBe(false);\n+  });\n+  it('limits area and skill learning to assigned units and activity level', async () => {\n+    const [area] = await resolveConditionalAssignments([targetRow({ scope: 'area', mode: 'learn', difficulty: 'medium', area: 'quantifiers' })], 'student');\n+    expect(area.unitIds).toHaveLength(4);\n+    expect(area.unitIds).not.toContain('group-3');\n+    const [skill] = await resolveConditionalAssignments([targetRow({ scope: 'skill', mode: 'learn', difficulty: 'advanced', unitId: 'group-3' })], 'student');\n+    expect(skill.unitIds).toEqual(['group-3']);\n+    expect(conditionalAssignmentAllowsAction(skill, { unitId: 'group-3', action: 'guided' })).toBe(true);\n+    expect(conditionalAssignmentAllowsAction(skill, { unitId: 'group-3', action: 'practice', difficulty: 'advanced' })).toBe(true);\n+    expect(conditionalAssignmentAllowsAction(skill, { unitId: 'group-3', action: 'practice', difficulty: 'beginner' })).toBe(false);\n+    expect(conditionalAssignmentAllowsAction(skill, { unitId: 'group-4', action: 'lesson' })).toBe(false);\n+  });\n+  it('practice grants do not grant notes or guided activities', async () => {\n+    const [practice] = await resolveConditionalAssignments([targetRow({ scope: 'general', mode: 'practice', difficulty: 'medium' })], 'student');\n+    expect(practice.unitIds).toHaveLength(26);\n+    expect(conditionalAssignmentAllowsAction(practice, { unitId: 'group-1', action: 'lesson' })).toBe(false);\n+    expect(conditionalAssignmentAllowsAction(practice, { unitId: 'group-1', action: 'guided' })).toBe(false);\n+    expect(conditionalAssignmentAllowsAction(practice, { unitId: 'group-1', action: 'practice', difficulty: 'medium' })).toBe(true);\n+  });\n+  it('rejects other students, hidden, draft, archived, and future or invalid unlocks', async () => {\n+    const rows = [row({ student_id: 'other' }), row({ visible_to_student: false }), row({ status: 'draft' }),\n+      row({ status: 'archived' }), row({ unlock_at: '2030-01-01T00:00:00Z' }), row({ unlock_at: 'invalid' })];\n+    expect(await resolveConditionalAssignments(rows, 'student', Date.parse('2026-10-01'))).toEqual([]);\n+    expect(await resolveConditionalAssignments([row({ status: 'completed', unlock_at: '2026-01-01' })], 'student', Date.parse('2026-10-01'))).toHaveLength(1);\n+  });\n+  it('invalid authoritative metadata never falls back to a broad source route', async () => {\n+    expect(await resolveConditionalAssignments([targetRow({ scope: 'skill', mode: 'learn', difficulty: 'medium', unitId: 'missing' }), targetRow(null)], 'student')).toEqual([]);\n+  });\n+  it('supports valid legacy links but rejects unsupported or malformed scopes', async () => {\n+    const legacy = (source_id: string) => row({ source_id, metadata_json: { bootcamp_id: 'conditionals' } });\n+    const [skill] = await resolveConditionalAssignments([legacy('/bootcamp/conditionals/practice?level=advanced&unit=group-3')], 'student');\n+    expect(skill.unitIds).toEqual(['group-3']);\n+    expect(skill.mode).toBe('practice');\n+    expect(await async () => {\n+    const own = targetRow({ scope: 'skill', mode: 'practice', difficulty: 'medium', unitId: 'group-3' });\n+    own.source_id = '/bootcamp/conditionals?start=orientation';\n+    const [assigned] = await resolveConditionalAssignments([own], 'student');\n+    expect(assigned.unitIds).toEqual(['group-3']);\n+    expect(conditionalAssignmentAllowsAction(assigned, { unitId: 'orientation', action: 'practice', difficulty: 'medium' })).toBe(false);\n+  });\n+});\n*** End Patch"));

## assistant

Implemented trusted Conditional assignment reading in three new files.

Students receive only their visible, unlocked, valid assigned targets. Invalid metadata fails closed. Legacy valid links remain supported. Action checks enforce assigned units, learning/practice mode, and activity level. Admins receive a separate full-course preview.

Seven tests passed; diff check clean. No database writes, migrations, or commits.
